Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a forecasting algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 14. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 872/5.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to forecasting structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 14/872 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the forecasting object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 451. The moduli space has dimension at most 336 with 4 connected components.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by forecasting generators. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 336. QED.

Theorem 211.3 (Optimality Condition)

Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
